## 2. THE CAMPAIGN STATE, WHOLE

**The object.** The global section `𝒮 = ⊗′_v S̄_v` — the restricted product of
the local Sonin closures over the places of ℚ, glued along the `E₁`-units, with
`F = ⊗F_v`, `F² = parity`, `F⁴ = 1`. It has no realization as a space of adelic
functions: a Hilbert object, not a function space.

**The two legs.** *Vanilla:* the programme's own kernels, finite instances
decided in Lean v4.29.1 with no Mathlib and no axioms — ### **304 Core terminals,
every one printing "does not depend on any axioms"** (b154: 271 → 275; b155: 275 → 279;
b156: 279 → 281; b158: 281 → 282; b159: 282 → 284; ### **b166: 284 → 288; b168: 288 → 292; b170: 292 → 296; b171: 296 → 299; b173: 299 → 302; b176: 302 → 304**). *Mathlib-facing:*
declared interfaces at the classical profile {propext, Classical.choice,
Quot.sound}, never load-bearing.

**Where the identity lane stands.** The apportionment is **closed-at-bench,
open-at-derivation**. Its interior has been reduced, across three acts, to a
single question:

- **b109** — the deviation equals the raw trace's even-share excess minus the
  ε-integral's. *Derived, exact.*
- **b115** — those two excesses are not two objects: they collapse into **one
  fixed density Ψ carrying no window parameter**, integrated against one
  a-dependent weight, and the window being a pure dilation gives
  **N(L) = 2∫₀²K(σ)Ψ(Lσ)dσ**, a fixed-kernel scale-average. *Derived; verified
  against the instrument's own values at 2.91e−16.* The arch column **divides
  out** of the existence-and-uniqueness question.
- **b116** — Φ_K := d/dσ[σK(σ)] is **mean-zero with exactly one sign change,
  positive then negative** (σ = 0.6215), whence **Ψ monotone increasing ⇒
  dN/dL ≥ 0 ⇒ N monotone ⇒ the balanced window is unique.** *Derived.*
- **b177** — ### **the b123 trim table printed whole and re-verified, and citation batch one.**
  *11 of 11 backfill rows verified by md5, 16 of 16 successor paths present;* ### **the four
  DO-NOT-TRIM rows are not re-verified and cannot be** — *the executor cannot fingerprint what he
  cannot read.* ### **THE AUTHOR'S PROJECT UI ACTION REMAINS THE SOLE DELETION PATH.**
  *Batch one: eight attempted,* ### **six CONFIRMED-AT-CONTENT, two UNRESOLVED** *(`HC^ar`,
  `SUS` — both object shorthands, with what was tried written beside each).* ### **No new
  bibliography entry was needed; all six resolved onto works already filed.** ***Backlog:
  111 remaining, owner the author.***
- **b176** — ### **the thirty-sixth seam closed; keystone v2.36, §41 *The external arc*.** *Two
  document-blocked questions settled at the PDF:* ### **the `R2` domain is SETTLED-TWO-SIDED and
  b36's registered factor 2 is confirmed correct** — *though a settled domain is not a settled
  implementation, and `R2`'s grade does not rise by itself* — ### **and `Lemma 5.2`'s domain
  `(1,2]` is confirmed exactly, the re-derivation's "NOT ON DISK" reason gone while the
  re-derivation stands at its grade.** ***The two forms agree TERM FOR TERM once `D_u` is
  expanded; the one divergence is in the POINTERS — (85)/(100) against (84)/(98), a version
  difference.*** Core 302 → 304. ### **The 117 unresolved references are carried forward as a
  standing item with the author as owner.**
- **b175** — ### **the citation sweep.** *266 live documents enumerated;* ### **44 shorthands,
  76 attributed numbered results, 7 non-key pointers.* **Resolved: six keys cited live and never
  filed** *(Ford; Guth–Maynard; Bui–Conrey–Young; Kowalski; Sekatskii; Broucke)*, ### **and the
  four `"CC's"` numbered results — `Lemma 5.2`, `Thm 4.7`, `Lemma 5.4`, `Prop 5.5` — ALL RESOLVE
  TO `2006.13771`.** ***So the apportionment lane, the ε-series lane and the h2 line all cite the
  same work and not one of them named it.*** ### **The `"CC's"` unnamed objects stay UNRESOLVED,
  left visibly so.** *Two findings filed: b36's R2 rode an unnamed Thm 4.7 whose domain caveat is
  now answerable; and the `Lemma 5.2` re-derivation's reason has gone.* ### **And the sweep
  contaminated its own re-run — its filings quote the very patterns it counts — so the counts of
  record are the enumeration's.**
- **b174** — ### **the shipped rows corrected to what the PDF established.** *The bibliography
  entry's grade rises to* ### **CONFIRMED-AT-CONTENT**, *the `UNVERIFIED` markings are withdrawn,
  the agreement is strengthened to* ### **term by term**, *and every correction leaves the
  original wording visible and dated.* ### **Two laws minted: invoking one result of a work does
  not import another result's hypotheses; and a renderer's silence is not the source's silence**
  — ***with the practice consequence that an external read recorded as ABSENCE is done at the
  source document, not at a rendering of it.***
- **b173** — ### **the scope condition decided SATISFIED, and four statements of a shipped
  report corrected.** *The interval hypothesis belongs to `Proposition 5.5`; the corpus uses

- **b171** — ### **the warrant's source read externally, and the principal product was a

  agrees with the published `22.9965` to every digit it prints.** Applicability: **CONFIRMS** —

- **b170** — ### **THE SEPARATION SEAL: the class of member-separating statements expressible in
  the requirements' own vocabulary is EMPTY.** *Every owner-expressible quantity is a linear
  functional of the per-mode terms, so a statement separates members only through `Σ_n c_n u_n`;
  constant `c` cannot see the member.* ### **The renamed-conclusion gate fired on half of it and
  the half is carried as inherited from b154/b158.** ### **The load-bearing step is R4 — a
  reading of the owner's phrase, not a theorem** — and the seal is over a **documented**
  vocabulary, not a mathematical space. ***A seal is not a ruling.*** Core 292 → 296.
- **b169** — ### **the thirty-fifth seam absorbed and route B's grounds READ.** *Keystone
  v2.35, §40.* ### **The record offers NOTHING USABLE toward defining the residual's split:**
  six candidate grounds located at content — one fixes the wrong part, one is closed twice
  over, one has no owner, one is a different split entirely, one is a renamed conclusion, and
  ### **the sixth is a read of the cited source that has never been performed.** ***Surviving
  that filter is not a recommendation.*** **Route B's prerequisite stands as b155 stated it.**
- **b168** — ### **the ε grid's left endpoint REPAIRED and the family verdict DECIDED.** *The
  routine was never at fault: b38's own `per_mode_eps_grids` returns zero at `ρ = 1` by its
  empty-range branch, and nobody had ever asked it.* The repair prepends `ρ = 1`, ### **leaving
  every incumbent node in place**, and its only footprint on the record is `−1.42226e-11` on
  `Ψ_W(0)` — **predicted before the run** and equal to `φ_e(0)`, the residue that hid the
  defect. ### **VERDICT: the one-sign property holds on a HALF-LINE, `μ > 0.48370`** — b38's
  member and `μ = 1` inside, the free end `μ = 0` outside. ### **A member inside is not thereby
  right; a member outside is not thereby wrong.** Core 288 → 292.
- **b167** — ### **the delta act HALTED at its own fourth gate.** *The three gates the ferry
  asked for passed; a gate this seat added — the derived answer `Δ(0) = 10` — failed by six
  orders.* ### **The cause: the ε grid's first node at `exp(1e-4)` and `np.interp` clamping
  below it**, identical at four resolutions — ***refinement cannot cure a domain defect.***
  ### **Ψ was protected by a t-proportional cancellation that is not a property of the
  instrument; `Δ` was the first raw-sum object ever asked for.** No prior result impugned.
- **b166** — ### **the dependence audit: the lane's whole member-dependence is ONE AFFINE
  DIRECTION**, `Ψ_μ = Ψ_σ + (σ_even − μ)·Δ`. *Invariant:* the collapse, the scale-average form,
  the arch column's dividing out, both derivative forms, b116's orientation **implication**,

  sign. ### **And b109's reduction splits on which target is held fixed** — `σ_even` plays two

- **b164** — ### **the census completed and the index keyed.** *All ten unread rows read at content:* ### **every grade locatable, three carrying none BY DESIGN, and four carrying `DERIVES` at a shifted column because their statement cells hold unescaped pipes** — *filed as a finding, the rows not edited.* ### **b163's “eleven” was ten** — this record's own miscount, corrected. *(m4) re-scored WRONG.*
- **b163** — ### **the findings reduced to eight**, by object rather than by chronology. ***The principal finding is a dependence, not a result:*** ### **the density lane's chain rests on b109's reduction, which was derived from b38's definition alone — one member of the family b154 named** — *so the chain is member-conditional and its own records do not say so.* ### **A scope location, not a re-grade, and not an argument for any member.** *One unifying statement refused as materially the register sentence.*
- **b162** — ### **the laws reduced to eight**, with *a result carries its conditions* accepted as a parent and ### **three proposed parents refused by the gate**. ***Two prior verdicts corrected by returning to founding texts*** — canon XVIII is the **eloquence line**, and b159's NEW-MEMBER verdict on the ease-of-phrasing law is ### **reversed to INSTANCE-OF**. *No mathematics; nothing promoted, demoted or re-graded by being integrated.*
- **b161** — ### **item 1 is PRESENTED WHOLE** at the loom: the family, both void-gate-satisfied endpoints, what is known (with the PHYSICAL verdict's conditionality verbatim), ### **what is not known (`R_N` UNDEFINED at owner grade, not merely unmeasured; the T-reading still at the grade barrier)**, and **four options, unranked**. ***No recommendation; nothing decided by being presented.*** *And the decision register gives every pending ruling one home* — ### **a decision presented in installments is a decision the record has not presented.**
- **b160** — ### **the research is EXHAUSTED short of a definitional ruling.** Every read-route is closed (gauge b155; the objects' own requirements b158; the arity bridge b159). ### **Route A and route B are both the author's and neither is derivable** — *the record says so rather than leaving the impression another act might turn it up.* ***No mathematics this act: it names and cures a class of failure — A READING NOT DONE — and does not advance the lane.***
- **b159** — ### **the E₁/even bridge DERIVES** (b35's registration, banked 2026-08-18: `ξ_n = √2·ψ_{2n}`, so the sector index is `2n mod 4` and lands only in `{0,2}` — ### **the `±i` sectors are unoccupied and b38 split exactly the two that remain**), ### **and it still does not constrain**, `proj4_sum` being a **completeness** statement and completeness being the void gate. ***Item 1 unmoved; the last read-route is CLOSED rather than BLOCKED.*** *A correction of b154's marking and of one of b158's two grounds, filed as a correction.* *Derived; decided at instances in Core.*
- **b158** — ### **and file E's own requirements do NOT pick a member.** Definedness is vacuous (every term typed `ℝ`); conjugation is a tautology (b104's argument was *built not to need* the identification, which is why it cannot supply one); cross-sector conditions are share-independent (a **corollary of b154**, not new); and kernel invariance is blocked by an **arity mismatch** — ### **`proj4_sum` decomposes into FOUR sectors where b38 splits TWO**, bridged only by the E₁/even READING. ***Four would-be criteria were gate-rejected as renamings, three of them naming the free end — because that member is characterised by ABSENCES, and the ease of phrasing a criterion is not evidence for the member it names.*** ### **The freedom is DEFINITIONAL at owner grade.** *Derived; decided at instances in Core.*
- **b156** — ### **the T-reading CONSUMES an apportionment, and the apportionment-free alternative is a FAMILY MEMBER, not an escape.** The even-sector sum of the per-mode raw traces minus the even-sector ε-parts is formable with its owners and is purely object-side, ### **but it equals `W₊(0)`** — the member charging the whole residual to the odd sector. ***So ν's physicality is not moot and route A is still owed, its target sharpened: the ruling chooses within a family containing both `μ = σ_even` and `μ = 0`.*** ### **And the certified ledger column enters the closed equation TWICE** — an input to the apportioned object side as well as the ledger side — ***the entries netting to `(1 − μ)` rather than cancelling.*** *Derived; decided at instances in Core.*
- **b155** — ### **that freedom is PHYSICAL, not gauge.** Exactly one of the identity's four terms carries the share and **nothing cancels** (the certified column is an **input** to b38's definition, not an output), so `d(D_closed)/dμ = +resid_N` — ***the same derivative from both of b38's printed forms.*** Hence **at most one share admits the identity at a cell**. ### **And the freedom cannot account for the recorded difference:** the share that would zero it differs at every cell (spread **0.1964**) and **exceeds 1 everywhere**, so it is not a share at all. ***Route C closed; route A's target stated and left to the author; route B given a number to beat and a newly visible prerequisite — the split is UNDEFINED at owner grade, not merely unmeasured.*** *Conditional throughout on b107's undischarged T-reading. Derived; decided at instances in Core.*
- **b154** — ### **the apportionment itself is not pinned.** Under every owner-quotable requirement the admissible apportionments form a **one-parameter family**, `W₊ = Σ_even a − Σ_even b − σ_even·D_N − ν·R_N`, with **b38's definition the member `ν = σ_even`** and **ν unconstrained**. The void gate is **exactly the normalization** and forces nothing else. ***A second, INDEPENDENT obstruction beside b109's — neither implies the other, and item 1 needs both.*** The freedom's size is **undetermined by the record**: `R_N` has no recorded value anywhere. *Derived, exact; decided at instances in Core.*
- **b117** — the enclosure runs. **The scale-average is strictly negative at
  every tested L across the licensed range, by a margin some four thousand
  times the measured floor** — so the one-sign property holds. ***The grade is
  exact and conditional: rigorous over the quadrature, conditional on samples
  whose error the record does not bound. It is NOT certified-numerics. The one
  blocker is a rigorous error bound for the prolate layer; and sixty points
  sample a range, they do not certify it.*** **And the sufficient condition is
  REFUTED: Ψ is not monotone increasing**, 149 descents all above the
  2.774e−05 scale. ***The fact holds and the mechanism that would explain it
  does not.***
- **b119** — **the mechanism is derived at enclosure grade.** A decomposition
  fixed in the registration and never revisited: ψ_coarse = the density
  convolved with the channel's own kernel at its own width, ψ_fine the
  remainder. The coarse part carries **56.8%** of the rise; the coarse pairing
  is **strictly negative at every cell**; the two pairings **sum exactly** to
  b117's scale-average; and the remainder is **dominated at 9 of 9 cells and 60
  of 60 sweep points, worst ratio 0.462.** ***THE SIGN'S REASON: coarse-trend
  dominance through a fine-suppressing channel — quantified, not asserted, the
  two parts comparable in amplitude (0.593 vs 0.572) and their pairings a factor
  of 2.2–3.5 apart.*** **Three limits travel with it: conditional on the
  samples, sampled not continuous, margin about a factor of two.**
- **b118** — the seam absorbs it and names the species. **THE THIRD OUTCOME:
  true conclusion under refuted hypothesis** — the implication stays a true
  theorem, the conclusion banks at its own evidence grade and borrows no credit
  from it, and the mechanism question opens by name. **THE DOMINANCE CANDIDATE**
  is staged as the next act's target, at **synthesis grade**: the density's
  coarse rise plus the channel's fine-suppression suggest the trend dominates
  the oscillation in the mean-zero average — *two facts pointing the right way,
  which is not yet a derivation.* What would make it one: **the remainder's
  kernel-pairing bounded above and the trend's bounded below.**

**The named opens, on the identity's path.** The one-sign question's
*mechanism*; the arch column's non-vanishing on the family (bench, not
derived); the T-reading's completion; the translation re-rerun (**held**); the
boundary license (**guarded**, three tags on the value clause).
**In the absolute-claims lane, at full force:** the orientation fact; the
dictionary question. **Standing across both:** the (L-identity) at complete
roster (row 24 — `h2`); the general-q full Parseval; the α and β steps and the
assembly-reading residue; the off-ray operator sense; the located law's one
bench step; the analytic identification; the canon-XIX-barred H-COH-∞ (re-grade
**held**); the general-ℚ reciprocity compile; the odd-place colimits.

**The standing laws this campaign minted.** Reproduce-before-extend (any
instrument rebuild reproduces its banked cells at recorded tolerance first);
the probe convention (pre-registration probes lawful **iff** disclosed with
outputs embedded); grade-indexed status; the interpolation-floor lesson
(derived data inherits its interpolant's floor; every claim states its scale);
the snapshot convention (amended on its first use to LF-normalized bytes);
the superlative ledger, with superlatives entering at shipping; **the
third-outcome cell** (true conclusion under refuted hypothesis, with its reading
pre-committed); **the verification law's self-application corollary** — run a new convention
against its own first artifact in the act that mints it; **a check claimed is a
check run** — any claimed check carries its artifact or is not claimed;
**author catches cite the conversation layer**; **a replacement must be better
where the answer is known**; **replacing a component is the only test that
isolates it**; **a diagnosis that has not survived a replacement is a suspicion,
not a cause**; and ### **A NULL CONFIGURATION LOCATES WHAT A REPLACEMENT CANNOT**
— *replacement varies **how** a component acts and can only exclude; a
configuration in which the component does nothing at all varies **whether** it
acts, and can locate.* **Its corollary, minted with it and worth more than it:
b124's three replacements were the expensive route and b126's null test was
nearly free — one column of an array already computed. The cheap test was
available the whole time and was not run, because the record reached for better
instruments before asking what the existing one did with the suspected component
absent.** Two further additions this seam makes: ### **A SHARED INPUT IS INVISIBLE
TO REPLACEMENT TESTING** — *a replacement panel measures only what its members do
not have in common* — and ### **AN INSTRUMENT'S INPUT INVENTORY IS A RESEARCH
OBJECT** — *which knobs exist is found incompletely by looking, not read off the
code once and filed; three axes have now been discovered after the instrument
entered production use, and the record does not assert the third is the last.*
### **SEVERAL AXES MEASURED SEPARATELY ARE NOT A COMBINED FLOOR** — the sequel to
the floor-axis law. And ### **A FIGURE TRAVELS WITH ITS QUANTITY OR IT DOES NOT
TRAVEL** — the same-name-different-quantity hazard, two instances on record
(b126's reconciliation target caught mid-act, b128's gate reference caught before
computing), *and the floor-axis law, the convergence-quantity rule and this one
should be read as one requirement rather than three.*
**And a limitation found in a standing law rather than a new law: REPRODUCE-
BEFORE-EXTEND IS A GATE AGAINST REGRESSION, NOT AGAINST ERROR** — b126 passed it
and failed u-convergence in the same act, on the same instrument.
**The later seams add:** ### **A PAIRING IS NAMED BY ITS KERNEL**; ### **A BUILD VERIFIES AGAINST ITS MANIFEST *AND* ITS MANIFEST'S SOURCE HEAD**; ### **A RESULT IS DISTINGUISHED FROM A TRUNCATION ARTIFACT BY FLATNESS UNDER REFINEMENT, NOT BY SIZE**; ### **A WITHDRAWAL SAYS THE EVIDENCE FAILED; A REFUTATION SAYS THE CLAIM IS FALSE**; ### **A CONVENTION THAT CAN LOSE AN AXIS ON EVIDENCE IS WORKING**; and ### **A SURVEY IS NOT A PREDICATE** — *a survey can only REMOVE candidates; it cannot produce what a licence requires, so a thorough negative read looks like progress precisely in proportion to its thoroughness*; ### **A PRESUPPOSITION THAT COMES TRUE IS NOT A PREDICTION THAT LANDED**; ### **A COUNT THAT COMES OUT EVEN IS NOT EVIDENCE THAT THE RIGHT THINGS WERE COUNTED**; ### **CONVERGENT DISCIPLINE AND TRANSMITTED DISCIPLINE LOOK IDENTICAL FROM THE OUTSIDE** — *a shared source is not transmission*; ### **A COMPLIANCE ASSERTION IS A FALSE PASS WITH A SIGNATURE ON IT** — *the check and the thing checked are the same object*; ### **A HABIT'S SAFETY ASSUMPTIONS ARE PART OF ITS SPECIFICATION**; ### **AN APPROXIMATION MARK IN A LIVE LEDGER ROW IS A DEBT** — *it resolves to a read or the row carries UNVERIFIED; a blank cell is visibly missing and gets filled, a MARKED cell looks filled*; ### **AN ARTIFACT IS NAMED BY ITS FILED RECORD, NOT BY THE LABEL A LEDGER USES FOR IT**; ### **A CHECK'S SPECIES INCLUDES ITS FAILURE POLARITY** — *a check that HAS falsely passed carries that fact thereafter*; ### **OWN-CORPUS-FIRST: INTERNAL CITATION AT CONTENT, OR NOTHING** — *a law true of mathematics but not read in this corpus would pass every check this record owns and still be an import wearing its clothes*; ### **PRECISION IS REFUTABILITY** — *a vague instance cannot be refused, only graded partial; a synthesis that collects only vague instances can never be wrong and is worth nothing*; ### **A CHECK'S FIRST RUN IS PART OF ITS CONSTRUCTION, NOT PART OF ITS SERVICE** — *because its verdict is **uninterpreted either way**, not because it is usually wrong: five false alarms and one real catch, and a fixture is what tells them apart*; ### **A CERTIFICATE IS ABOUT AN OBJECT, AND OUTWARD ROUNDING DOES NOT REPAIR A SUBSTITUTED ONE — IT DRESSES IT**; ### **A JUSTIFICATION ARRIVING AFTER A RULING IS NOT EVIDENCE FOR THE RULING**; ### **A SMOOTHED QUANTITY'S VALIDATED DOMAIN IS ITS SOURCE'S VALIDATED DOMAIN SHRUNK BY THE SMOOTHING WIDTH** — *convolution does not preserve a domain; it consumes one*; ### **A CERTIFIED NUMERIC IS A PROOF OF NOTHING EXCEPT THE NUMERIC**; ### **A BENCH NUMBER MAY CONSTRAIN REQUIRED PRECISION WITHOUT SUPPLYING EVIDENCE** — *the guard's first permissive cell*; ### **A PREDICATE MET IN ITS WORDS BUT NOT IN ITS INTENT IS NOT MET** — the licence law's completion, *a licence re-read against predicate **and** purpose*; ### **A STAGING LICENCE CARRIES ITS PREDICATE, AND A CLEARED BLOCKER DOES NOT REVIVE A REFUTED LICENCE** — *when a dependency's ground changes, downstream licences are re-read rather than inherited.* ***The last two and the stale-build law are siblings: internal consistency is not currency, and an artifact must be checked against what it was derived from.***

**The guard, standing over everything numerical:** *noticed is not earned.* The
balanced window's **location** is banked at **zero interpretive weight**, and so
is every resemblance the record has noticed — including the wall-species kinship
and the critical-line shape-kinship, both filed at synthesis grade behind their
fences.
